HUTCN(
  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (dat): DAT(
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-2): 3 x SWDABlock(
            (norm1): LayerNorm2d(
              (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (norm2): LayerNorm2d(
              (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (norm3): LayerNorm2d(
              (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (w_attn): WindowAttention(
              (qkv): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=288, bias=True)
              (proj): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=96, bias=True)
            )
            (sw_attn): WindowAttention(
              (qkv): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=288, bias=True)
              (proj): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=96, bias=True)
            )
            (ffn): ConvFFN(
              (pw1): Conv2d(96, 192, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
              (dw): Conv2d(192, 192, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=192)
              (pw2): Conv2d(192, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
              (act): GELU(approximate='none')
            )
          )
        )
        (norm): LayerNorm2d(
          (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
        )
      )
      (conv3): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (cnn): Sequential(
        (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (3): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (4): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (5): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (6): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      )
    )
    (tecn2): TECN(
      (elan): ELAN(
        (patch_embed): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-2): 3 x ELAB(
            (shift_conv1): ShiftConv(
              (conv): Conv2d(480, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            )
            (shift_conv2): ShiftConv(
              (conv): Conv2d(480, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            )
            (gmsa): GMSA(
              (qkv_list): ModuleList(
                (0-2): 3 x Linear(in_features=96, out_features=288, bias=False)
              )
              (attn_drop_list): ModuleList(
                (0-2): 3 x Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              )
              (proj_list): ModuleList(
                (0-2): 3 x Linear(in_features=96, out_features=96, bias=True)
              )
              (proj_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (relative_position_bias_tables): ParameterList(
                  (0): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 121x2 (cuda:0)]
                  (1): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 529x2 (cuda:0)]
                  (2): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 2209x2 (cuda:0)]
              )
            )
            (norm1): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (norm2): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (mlp): Sequential(
              (0): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=384, bias=True)
              (1): GELU(approximate='none')
              (2): Linear(in_features=384, out_features=96, bias=True)
              (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
            )
            (drop_path): Identity()
          )
        )
        (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      )
      (conv3): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (cnn): Sequential(
        (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (3): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (4): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (5): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (6): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      )
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
  )
  (pixel_shuffle): PixelShuffle(upscale_factor=4)
  (recon_conv1): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
  (recon_conv2): Conv2d(96, 48, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
)
HUTCN(
  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

              )

                  (1): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 529x2 (cuda:0)]
                  (2): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 2209x2 (cuda:0)]
              )
            )
            (norm1): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (norm2): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (mlp): Sequential(
              (0): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=384, bias=True)
              (1): GELU(approximate='none')
              (2): Linear(in_features=384, out_features=96, bias=True)
              (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
            )
            (drop_path): Identity()

      )
      (conv3): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (cnn): Sequential(
        (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
        (2): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      )
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(

Model(
  (model): HUTCN(
    (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (post_unet_esa): ESA(
      (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
      (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
      (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
    )

    (B1): P_HTCB(
      (tesa_in): TESA(

        (conv3): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (cnn): Sequential(
          (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
          (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
          (2): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        )
      )

        (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
      )

          (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
          (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

          (sigmoid): Sigmoid()

        )
        (esa2): ESA(

          (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

          (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
          (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
        )
        (esa3): ESA(
          (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
          (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
          (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

          (sigmoid): Sigmoid()

          (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
          (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
